floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, \n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, \n                   const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm\n    // For each intermediate node k\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        // For each source node i\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n            // For each destination node j\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n                \n                const unsigned int newDist = distIK + distKJ;\n                \n                if (newDist < distIJ) {\n                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;\n                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n    \n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n                \n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\", \n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Allocate matrices\n    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(numNodes * numNodes);\n    std::vector<unsigned int> path(numNodes * numNodes);\n    \n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes);\n    \n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate operations per second\n    // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n    double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation (integer hash-based)\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results_int(dist, \"DistanceMatrix\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(dist, numNodes);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\ncmake_minimum_required(VERSION   // rows per rank\n    std::vector<int> offsets;  // first row per rank\n};\n\nstatic RowBlocks computeRowBlocks(const size_t numNodes, const int numRanks) {\n    RowBlocks b;\n    b.counts.resize(numRanks);\n    b.offsets.resize(numRanks);\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    size_t off = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n        const size_t c = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < rem ? 1u : 0u);\n        b.counts[r] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        b.offsets[r] = static_cast<int>(off);\n        off += c;\n    }\n    return b;\n}\n\n// Owner rank of a given global row, given the block decomposition above.\nstatic inline int ownerOfRow(const size_t row, const size_t numNodes, const int numRanks) {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t fat = (base + 1) * rem;  // rows covered by the ranks with one extra row\n    if (row < fat) {\n        return static_cast<int>(row / (base + 1));\n    }\n    return static_cast<int>(rem + (row - fat) / base);\n}\n\n// Generates exactly the same values as the sequential initialization, but only\n// stores the rows owned by this rank.\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t rowStart, const size_t rowCount,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    const size_t first = rowStart * numNodes;\n    const size_t last = (rowStart + rowCount) * numNodes;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {\n        const unsigned int v = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n        if (i >= first && i < last) {\n            dist[i - first] = v;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < rowCount; ++r) {\n        const size_t g = rowStart + r;\n        dist[r * numNodes + g] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n// The sequential initialization assigns path[row][col] = row for every entry.\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,\n                          const size_t rowStart, const size_t rowCount) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < rowCount; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict p = path.data() + r * numNodes;\n        const unsigned int v = static_cast<unsigned int>(rowStart + r);\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n            p[j] = v;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Relaxes one local row against the (already available) pivot row k.\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict rowK, const size_t numNodes,\n                            const unsigned int k) {\n    const unsigned int distIK = d[k];\n    // Branch-free so the compiler can vectorize the update with masked selects.\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + rowK[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = d[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        d[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        p[j] = better ? k : p[j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed Floyd-Warshall: for every pivot k the owning rank broadcasts row\n// k, all ranks then relax their own rows against it.  The pivot row of the next\n// iteration is computed first and its broadcast is started early so that the\n// communication overlaps the bulk of the local work.\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes,\n                   const size_t rowStart, const size_t rowCount,\n                   const int numRanks) {\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> bufA(numNodes), bufB(numNodes);\n    unsigned int* curBuf = bufA.data();\n    unsigned int* nextBuf = bufB.data();\n    const int count = static_cast<int>(numNodes);\n\n    const size_t rowEnd = rowStart + rowCount;\n    auto isMine = [&](const size_t row) { return row >= rowStart && row < rowEnd; };\n\n    // Prime the pipeline with the very first pivot row.\n    MPI_Request req;\n    {\n        const int owner = ownerOfRow(0, numNodes, numRanks);\n        if (isMine(0)) {\n            std::memcpy(curBuf, dist.data() + (0 - rowStart) * numNodes, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(curBuf, count, MPI_UNSIGNED, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &req);\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        MPI_Wait(&req, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int ku = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);\n\n        const size_t kNext = k + 1;\n        const bool haveNext = kNext < numNodes;\n        const int nextOwner = haveNext ? ownerOfRow(kNext, numNodes, numRanks) : 0;\n        const bool nextIsMine = haveNext && isMine(kNext);\n\n        // Compute the next pivot row first, then kick off its broadcast so the\n        // remaining local rows are relaxed while the message is in flight.\n        if (nextIsMine) {\n            const size_t lr = kNext - rowStart;\n            relaxRow(dist.data() + lr * numNodes, path.data() + lr * numNodes, curBuf, numNodes, ku);\n            std::memcpy(nextBuf, dist.data() + lr * numNodes, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n        if (haveNext) {\n            MPI_Ibcast(nextBuf, count, MPI_UNSIGNED, nextOwner, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &req);\n        }\n\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < rowCount; ++r) {\n            if (nextIsMine && rowStart + r == kNext) {\n                continue;  // already done above\n            }\n            relaxRow(dist.data() + r * numNodes, path.data() + r * numNodes, curBuf, numNodes, ku);\n        }\n\n        std::swap(curBuf, nextBuf);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help &numRanks);\n\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CemDTm2qFaM2GXrWzQTCK","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017n65wrCBUAm7A4DpcQo9ap","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/floydwarshall_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_mpi_r3/floydwarshall/floydwarshall.cpp","content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;\nconstexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n// The global matrix is stored row-major: entry (row, col) lives at row * n + col,\n// i.e. the sequential code's dist[idx2(j, i, n)] is source i / destination j.\n//\n// Parallelization: the matrix is distributed over a 2D process grid of\n// pr x pc ranks; rank (r, c) owns the contiguous row block r and column block c.\n// For every pivot k, the process row owning k broadcasts its slice of row k down\n// the process columns and the process column owning k broadcasts its slice of\n// column k along the process rows.  Both messages are only n/pc resp. n/pr\n// elements long and involve only pr resp. pc ranks, which keeps the\n// communication cost far below a 1D row decomposition at high rank counts.\n// The pivot data of iteration k+1 is produced first and its broadcasts are\n// posted before the bulk of the local update, so communication overlaps compute.\n\n// Contiguous block decomposition of `numNodes` items over `parts` parts.\nstruct Blocks {\n    std::vector<int> counts;\n    std::vector<int> offsets;\n\n    Blocks(const size_t numNodes, const int parts) : counts(parts), offsets(parts) {\n        const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n        const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n        size_t off = 0;\n        for (int p = 0; p < parts; ++p) {\n            const size_t c = base + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < rem ? 1u : 0u);\n            counts[p] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            offsets[p] = static_cast<int>(off);\n            off += c;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Part that owns a given global index.\n    int owner(const size_t index) const {\n        // Blocks are monotonically ordered, so a direct formula works.\n        const size_t parts = counts.size();\n        const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(offsets.back() + counts.back()) / parts;\n        const size_t rem = static_cast<size_t>(offsets.back() + counts.back()) % parts;\n        const size_t fat = (base + 1) * rem;  // covered by the parts with one extra item\n        if (index < fat) {\n            return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1));\n        }\n        return static_cast<int>(rem + (index - fat) / base);\n    }\n};\n\n// Generates exactly the same values as the sequential initialization, but only\n// stores the sub-block owned by this rank.\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t rowStart, const size_t rowCount,\n                              const size_t colStart, const size_t colCount,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    for (size_t row = 0; row < numNodes; ++row) {\n        const bool mine = (row >= rowStart && row < rowStart + rowCount);\n        if (!mine) {\n            // Still has to advance the RNG stream to stay bit-identical.\n            for (size_t col = 0; col < numNodes; ++col) {\n                (void)rand_r(&seed);\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n        unsigned int* __restrict d = dist.data() + (row - rowStart) * colCount;\n        for (size_t col = 0; col < numNodes; ++col) {\n            const unsigned int v =\n                rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n            if (col >= colStart && col < colStart + colCount) {\n                d[col - colStart] = v;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    const size_t dStart = std::max(rowStart, colStart);\n    const size_t dEnd = std::min(rowStart + rowCount, colStart + colCount);\n    for (size_t g = dStart; g < dEnd; ++g) {\n        dist[(g - rowStart) * colCount + (g - colStart)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n// The sequential initialization assigns path[row][col] = row for every entry.\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t rowStart,\n                          const size_t rowCount, const size_t colCount) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < rowCount; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict p = path.data() + r * colCount;\n        const unsigned int v = static_cast<unsigned int>(rowStart + r);\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < colCount; ++j) {\n            p[j] = v;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Relaxes one local row against the pivot row slice.\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict rowK, const size_t colCount,\n                            const unsigned int distIK, const unsigned int k) {\n    // Branch-free so the compiler can vectorize the update with masked selects.\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < colCount; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + rowK[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = d[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        d[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        p[j] = better ? k : p[j];\n    }\n}\n\nstruct Grid {\n    int pr = 1, pc = 1;      // process grid dimensions\n    int myRow = 0, myCol = 0;\n    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;  // ranks of the same process row (varying column)\n    MPI_Comm colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;  // ranks of the same process column (varying row)\n};\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes,\n                   const Blocks& rowBlocks, const Blocks& colBlocks,\n                   const Grid& grid) {\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t rowStart = static_cast<size_t>(rowBlocks.offsets[grid.myRow]);\n    const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(rowBlocks.counts[grid.myRow]);\n    const size_t colStart = static_cast<size_t>(colBlocks.offsets[grid.myCol]);\n    const size_t colCount = static_cast<size_t>(colBlocks.counts[grid.myCol]);\n\n    // Double buffers for the pivot row/column slices (current and prefetched).\n    std::vector<unsigned int> rowBuf(2 * colCount), colBuf(2 * rowCount);\n    unsigned int* curRow = rowBuf.data();\n    unsigned int* nextRow = rowBuf.data() + colCount;\n    unsigned int* curCol = colBuf.data();\n    unsigned int* nextCol = colBuf.data() + rowCount;\n\n    const int rowLen = static_cast<int>(colCount);\n    const int colLen = static_cast<int>(rowCount);\n\n    MPI_Request reqs[2];\n\n    // Fills the pivot slices for iteration `k` from local data (only the owning\n    // ranks contribute) and starts the two broadcasts.\n    auto postPivot = [&](const size_t k, unsigned int* rowDst, unsigned int* colDst) {\n        const int kr = rowBlocks.owner(k);\n        const int kc = colBlocks.owner(k);\n        if (grid.myRow == kr) {\n            std::memcpy(rowDst, dist.data() + (k - rowStart) * colCount,\n                        colCount * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n        if (grid.myCol == kc) {\n            const size_t lc = k - colStart;\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < rowCount; ++i) {\n                colDst[i] = dist[i * colCount + lc];\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(rowDst, rowLen, MPI_UNSIGNED, kr, grid.colComm, &reqs[0]);\n        MPI_Ibcast(colDst, colLen, MPI_UNSIGNED, kc, grid.rowComm, &reqs[1]);\n    };\n\n    postPivot(0, curRow, curCol);\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        MPI_Waitall(2, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int ku = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);\n\n        const size_t kNext = k + 1;\n        const bool haveNext = kNext < numNodes;\n        const int nr = haveNext ? rowBlocks.owner(kNext) : 0;\n        const int nc = haveNext ? colBlocks.owner(kNext) : 0;\n        const bool rowNextMine = haveNext && grid.myRow == nr;\n        const bool colNextMine = haveNext && grid.myCol == nc;\n        const size_t pivotLocalRow = rowNextMine ? kNext - rowStart : 0;\n\n        // Produce the pivot data of the next iteration first, so its broadcasts\n        // can be posted before the (much larger) bulk update.  Relaxation with a\n        // fixed pivot is idempotent, so the overlap between the row and the\n        // column update below is harmless.\n        if (rowNextMine) {\n            relaxRow(dist.data() + pivotLocalRow * colCount, path.data() + pivotLocalRow * colCount,\n                     curRow, colCount, curCol[pivotLocalRow], ku);\n            std::memcpy(nextRow, dist.data() + pivotLocalRow * colCount,\n                        colCount * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n        if (colNextMine) {\n            const size_t lc = kNext - colStart;\n            const unsigned int distKJ = curRow[lc];\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < rowCount; ++i) {\n                const size_t off = i * colCount + lc;\n                const unsigned int newDist = curCol[i] + distKJ;\n                if (newDist < dist[off]) {\n                    dist[off] = newDist;\n                    path[off] = ku;\n                }\n                nextCol[i] = dist[off];\n            }\n        }\n        if (haveNext) {\n            MPI_Ibcast(nextRow, rowLen, MPI_UNSIGNED, nr, grid.colComm, &reqs[0]);\n            MPI_Ibcast(nextCol, colLen, MPI_UNSIGNED, nc, grid.rowComm, &reqs[1]);\n        }\n\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < rowCount; ++i) {\n            if (rowNextMine && i == pivotLocalRow) {\n                continue;  // already relaxed above\n            }\n            relaxRow(dist.data() + i * colCount, path.data() + i * colCount, curRow, colCount,\n                     curCol[i], ku);\n        }\n\n        std::swap(curRow, nextRow);\n        std::swap(curCol, nextCol);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n Both messages are only n/pc resp. n/pr\n// elements long and involve only pr resp. pc ranks, which keeps the\n// communication cost far below a 1D row decomposition at high rank counts.\n// The pivot data of iteration k+1 is produced first and its broadcasts are\n// posted before the bulk of the local update, so communication overlaps compute.\n\n// Contiguous block decomposition of `numNodes` items over `parts` parts.\nstruct Blocks {\n    std::vector<int> counts;\n    std::vector<int> offsets;\n\n    Blocks(const size_t numNodes, const int parts) : counts(parts), offsets(parts) {\n        const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n        const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n        size_t off = 0;\n        for (int p = 0; p < parts; ++p) {\n            const size_t c = base + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < rem ? 1u : 0u);\n            counts[p] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            offsets[p] = static_cast<int>(off);\n            off += c;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Part that owns a given global index.\n    int owner(const size_t index) const {\n        // Blocks are monotonically ordered, so a direct formula works.\n        const size_t parts = counts.size();\n        const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(offsets.back() + counts.back()) / parts;\n        const size_t rem = static_cast<size_t>(offsets.back() + counts.back()) % parts;\n        const size_t fat = (base + 1) * rem;  // covered by the parts with one extra item\n        if (index < fat) {\n            return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1));\n        }\n        return static_cast<int>(rem + (index - fat) / base);\n    }\n};\n\n// Generates exactly the same values as the sequential initialization, but only\n// stores the sub-block owned by this rank.\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t rowStart, const size_t rowCount,\n                              const size_t colStart, const size_t colCount,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    for (size_t row = 0; row < numNodes; ++row) {\n        const bool mine = (row >= rowStart && row < rowStart + rowCount);\n        if (!mine) {\n            // Still has to advance the RNG stream to stay bit-identical.\n            for (size_t col = 0; col < numNodes; ++col) {\n                (void)rand_r(&seed);\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n        unsigned int* __restrict d = dist.data() + (row - rowStart) * colCount;\n        for (size_t col = 0; g < dEnd; ++g) {\n        dist[(g - rowStart) * colCount + (g - colStart)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n// The sequential initialization assigns path[row][col] = row for every entry.\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t rowStart,\n                          const size_t rowCount, const size_t colCount) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; ++j) {\n            p[j] = v;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Relaxes one local row against the pivot row slice.\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict rowK, const size_t colCount,\n                            const unsigned int distIK, const unsigned int k) {\n    // Branch-free so the compiler can vectorize the update with masked selects.\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < colCount; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + rowK[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = d[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        d[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        p[j] = better ? k : p[j];\n    }\n}\n\nstruct Grid {\n    int pr = 1, pc = 1;      // process grid dimensions\n    int myRow = 0, myCol = 0;\n    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;  // ranks of the same process row (varying column)\n    MPI_Comm colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;  // ranks of the same process column (varying row)\n};\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes,\n                   const Blocks& rowBlocks, const Blocks& colBlocks,\n                   const Grid& grid) {\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t rowStart = static_cast<size_t>(rowBlocks.offsets[grid.myRow]);\n    const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(rowBlocks.counts[grid.myRow]);\n    const size_t colStart = static_cast<size_t>(colBlocks.offsets[grid.myCol]);\n    const size_t colCount = static_cast<size_t>(colBlocks.counts[grid.myCol]);\n\n    // Double buffers for the pivot row/column slices (current and prefetched).\n    std::vector<unsigned int> rowBuf(2 * colCount), colBuf(2 * rowCount);\n    unsigned int* curRow = rowBuf.data();\n    unsigned int* nextRow = rowBuf.data() + colCount;\n    unsigned int* curCol = colBuf.data();\n    unsigned int* nextCol = colBuf.data() + rowCount;\n\n    const int rowLen = static_cast<int>(colCount);\n    const int colLen = static_cast<int>(rowCount);\n\n    MPI_Request reqs[2];\n\n    // Fills the pivot slices for iteration `k` from local data (only the owning\n    // ranks contribute) and starts the two broadcasts.\n    auto postPivot = [&](const size_t k, unsigned int* rowDst, unsigned int* colDst) {\n        const int kr = rowBlocks.owner(k);\n        const int kc = colBlocks.owner(k);\n        if (grid.myRow == kr) {\n            std::memcpy(rowDst, dist.data() + (k - rowStart) * colCount,\n                        colCount * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n        if (grid.myCol == kc) {\n            const size_t lc = k - colStart;\n            for (size_t i = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        MPI_Waitall(2, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int ku = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);\n\n        const size_t kNext = k + 1;\n        const bool haveNext = kNext < numNodes;\n        const int nr = haveNext ? rowBlocks.owner(kNext) : 0;\n        const int nc = haveNext ? colBlocks.owner(kNext) : 0;\n        const bool rowNextMine = haveNext && grid.myRow == nr;\n        const bool colNextMine = haveNext && grid.myCol == nc;\n        const size_t pivotLocalRow = rowNextMine ? kNext - rowStart : 0;\n\n        // Produce the pivot data of the next iteration first, so its broadcasts\n        // can be posted before the (much larger) bulk update.  Relaxation with a\n        // fixed pivot is idempotent, so the overlap between the row and the\n        // column update below is harmless.\n        if (rowNextMine) {\n            relaxRow(dist.data() + pivotLocalRow * colCount, path.data() + pivotLocalRow * colCount,\n                     curRow, colCount, curCol[pivotLocalRow], ku);\n            std::memcpy(nextRow, dist.data() + pivotLocalRow * colCount,\n                        colCount * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n        if (colNextMine) {\n            const size_t lc = kNext - colStart;\n            const unsigned int distKJ = curRow[lc];\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < rowCount; ++i) {\n                const size_t off = i * colCount + lc;\n                const unsigned int newDist = curCol[i] + distKJ;\n                if (newDist < dist[off]) {\n                    dist[off] = newDist;\n                    path[off] = ku;\n                }\n                nextCol[i] = dist[off];\n            }\n        }\n        if (haveNext) {\n            MPI_Ibcast(nextRow, rowLen, MPI_UNSIGNED, nr, grid.colComm, &reqs[0]);\n            MPI_Ibcast(nextCol, colLen, MPI_UNSIGNED, nc, grid.rowComm, &reqs[1]);\n        }\n\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < rowCount; ++i) {\n            if (rowNextMine && i == pivotLocalRow) {\n                continue;  // already relaxed above\n            }\n            relaxRow(dist.data() + i * colCount, path.data() + i * colCount, curRow, colCount,\n                     curCol[i], ku);\n        }\n\n        std::swap(curRow, nextRow);\n        std::swap(curCol, nextCol);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;\nconstexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;\n\n// Index calculation for flattened 2D array\ninline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t i, const size_t j, const size_t n) noexcept {\n    return j * n + i;\n}\n\n// The matrix is stored row-major: element (row, col) lives at row * n + col,\n// i.e. dist[idx2(j, i, n)] is the entry for source i / destination j.\n// The matrix is distributed by contiguous blocks of rows (sources) over the\n// MPI ranks, so every rank owns rowCount complete rows starting at rowStart.\n\nstruct RowBlocks {\n    std::vector<int> counts;   // rows per rank\n    std::vector<int> offsets;  // first row per rank\n};\n\nstatic RowBlocks computeRowBlocks(const size_t numNodes, const int numRanks) {\n    RowBlocks b;\n    b.counts.resize(numRanks);\n    b.offsets.resize(numRanks);\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    size_t off = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n        const size_t c = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < rem ? 1u : 0u);\n        b.counts[r] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        b.offsets[r] = static_cast<int>(off);\n        off += c;\n    }\n    return b;\n}\n\n// Owner rank of a given global row, given the block decomposition above.\nstatic inline int ownerOfRow(const size_t row, const size_t numNodes, const int numRanks) {\n    const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks);\n    const size_t fat = (base + 1) * rem;  // rows covered by the ranks with one extra row\n    if (row < fat) {\n        return static_cast<int>(row / (base + 1));\n    }\n    return static_cast<int>(rem + (row - fat) / base);\n}\n\n// Generates exactly the same values as the sequential initialization, but only\n// stores the rows owned by this rank.\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t rowStart, const size_t rowCount,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    const size_t first = rowStart * numNodes;\n    const size_t last = (rowStart + rowCount) * numNodes;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {\n        const unsigned int v = rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n        if (i >= first && i < last) {\n            dist[i - first] = v;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < rowCount; ++r) {\n        const size_t g = rowStart + r;\n        dist[r * numNodes + g] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n// The sequential initialization assigns path[row][col] = row for every entry.\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes,\n                          const size_t rowStart, const size_t rowCount) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < rowCount; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict p = path.data() + r * numNodes;\n        const unsigned int v = static_cast<unsigned int>(rowStart + r);\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n            p[j] = v;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Relaxes one local row against the (already available) pivot row k.\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict rowK, const size_t numNodes,\n                            const unsigned int k) {\n    const unsigned int distIK = d[k];\n    // Branch-free so the compiler can vectorize the update with masked selects.\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + rowK[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = d[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        d[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        p[j] = better ? k : p[j];\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed Floyd-Warshall: for every pivot k the owning rank broadcasts row\n// k, all ranks then relax their own rows against it.  The pivot row of the next\n// iteration is computed first and its broadcast is started early so that the\n// communication overlaps the bulk of the local work.\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes,\n                   const size_t rowStart, const size_t rowCount,\n                   const int numRanks) {\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<unsigned int> bufA(numNodes), bufB(numNodes);\n    unsigned int* curBuf = bufA.data();\n    unsigned int* nextBuf = bufB.data();\n    const int count = static_cast<int>(numNodes);\n\n    const size_t rowEnd = rowStart + rowCount;\n    auto isMine = [&](const size_t row) { return row >= rowStart && row < rowEnd; };\n\n    // Prime the pipeline with the very first pivot row.\n    MPI_Request req;\n    {\n        const int owner = ownerOfRow(0, numNodes, numRanks);\n        if (isMine(0)) {\n            std::memcpy(curBuf, dist.data() + (0 - rowStart) * numNodes, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(curBuf, count, MPI_UNSIGNED, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &req);\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        MPI_Wait(&req, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int ku = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);\n\n        const size_t kNext = k + 1;\n        const bool haveNext = kNext < numNodes;\n        const int nextOwner = haveNext ? ownerOfRow(kNext, numNodes, numRanks) : 0;\n        const bool nextIsMine = haveNext && isMine(kNext);\n\n        // Compute the next pivot row first, then kick off its broadcast so the\n        // remaining local rows are relaxed while the message is in flight.\n        if (nextIsMine) {\n            const size_t lr = kNext - rowStart;\n            relaxRow(dist.data() + lr * numNodes, path.data() + lr * numNodes, curBuf, numNodes, ku);\n            std::memcpy(nextBuf, dist.data() + lr * numNodes, numNodes * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n        if (haveNext) {\n            MPI_Ibcast(nextBuf, count, MPI_UNSIGNED, nextOwner, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &req);\n        }\n\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < rowCount; ++r) {\n            if (nextIsMine && rowStart + r == kNext) {\n                continue;  // already done above\n            }\n            relaxRow(dist.data() + r * numNodes, path.data() + r * numNodes, curBuf, numNodes, ku);\n        }\n\n        std::swap(curBuf, nextBuf);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d\\n\", numRanks);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    const RowBlocks blocks = computeRowBlocks(numNodes, numRanks);\n    const size_t rowStart = static_cast<size_t>(blocks.offsets[rank]);\n    const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(blocks.counts[rank]);\n\n    // Allocate the local row block of both matrices\n    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(rowCount * numNodes);\n    std::vector<unsigned int> path(rowCount * numNodes);\n\n    // Initialize\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    }\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, rowStart, rowCount, 1, MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, numNodes, rowStart, rowCount);\n\n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    }\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes, rowStart, rowCount, numRanks);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    long long elapsed = static_cast<long long>(duration.count());\n operations per second\n        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n        double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n        double gflops = ops / (elapsed / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    // Collect the full distance matrix on rank 0 for output / validation\n    std::vector<unsigned int> fullDist;\n    if (printResults || Both messages are only n/pc resp. n/pr\n// elements long and involve only pr resp. pc ranks, which keeps the\n// communication cost far below a 1D row decomposition at high rank counts.\n// The pivot data of iteration k+1 is produced first and its broadcasts are\n// posted before the bulk of the local update, so communication overlaps compute.\n\n// Contiguous block decomposition of `numNodes` items over `parts` parts.\nstruct Blocks {\n    std::vector<int> counts;\n    std::vector<int> offsets;\n\n    Blocks(const size_t numNodes, const int parts) : counts(parts), offsets(parts) {\n        const size_t base = numNodes / static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n        const size_t rem = numNodes % static_cast<size_t>(parts);\n        size_t off = 0;\n        for (int p = 0; p < parts; ++p) {\n            const size_t c = base + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < rem ? 1u : 0u);\n            counts[p] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            offsets[p] = static_cast<int>(off);\n            off += c;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Part that owns a given global index.\n    int owner(const size_t index) const {\n        // Blocks are monotonically ordered, so a direct formula works.\n        const size_t parts = counts.size();\n        const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(offsets.back() + counts.back()) / parts;\n        const size_t rem = static_cast<size_t>(offsets.back() + counts.back()) % parts;\n        const size_t fat = (base + 1) * rem;  // covered by the parts with one extra item\n        if (index < fat) {\n            return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1));\n        }\n        return static_cast<int>(rem + (index - fat) / base);\n    }\n};\n\n// Generates exactly the same values as the sequential initialization, but only\n// stores the sub-block owned by this rank.\nvoid initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes,\n                              const size_t rowStart, const size_t rowCount,\n                              const size_t colStart, const size_t colCount,\n                              const unsigned int rangeMin, const unsigned int rangeMax) {\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;\n\n    for (size_t row = 0; row < numNodes; ++row) {\n        const bool mine = (row >= rowStart && row < rowStart + rowCount);\n        if (!mine) {\n            // Still has to advance the RNG stream to stay bit-identical.\n            for (size_t col = 0; col < numNodes; ++col) {\n                (void)rand_r(&seed);\n            }\n            continue;\n        }\n        unsigned int* __restrict d = dist.data() + (row - rowStart) * colCount;\n        for (size_t col = 0; col < numNodes; ++col) {\n            const unsigned int v =\n                rangeMin + (unsigned int)(range * rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX);\n            if (col >= colStart && col < colStart + colCount) {\n                d[col - colStart] = v;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Set diagonal to zero (distance from node to itself is 0)\n    const size_t dStart = std::max(rowStart, colStart);\n    const size_t dEnd = std::min(rowStart + rowCount, colStart + colCount);\n    for (size_t g = dStart; g < dEnd; ++g) {\n        dist[(g - rowStart) * colCount + (g - colStart)] = 0;\n    }\n}\n\n// The sequential initialization assigns path[row][col] = row for every entry.\nvoid initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t rowStart,\n                          const size_t rowCount, const size_t colCount) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < rowCount; ++r) {\n        unsigned int* __restrict p = path.data() + r * colCount;\n        const unsigned int v = static_cast<unsigned int>(rowStart + r);\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < colCount; ++j) {\n            p[j] = v;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Relaxes one local row against the pivot row slice.\nstatic inline void relaxRow(unsigned int* __restrict d, unsigned int* __restrict p,\n                            const unsigned int* __restrict rowK, const size_t colCount,\n                            const unsigned int distIK, const unsigned int k) {\n    // Branch-free so the compiler can vectorize the update with masked selects.\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < colCount; ++j) {\n        const unsigned int newDist = distIK + rowK[j];\n        const unsigned int cur = d[j];\n        const bool better = newDist < cur;\n        d[j] = better ? newDist : cur;\n        p[j] = better ? k : p[j];\n    }\n}\n\nstruct Grid {\n    int pr = 1, pc = 1;      // process grid dimensions\n    int myRow = 0, myCol = 0;\n    MPI_Comm rowComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;  // ranks of the same process row (varying column)\n    MPI_Comm colComm = MPI_COMM_NULL;  // ranks of the same process column (varying row)\n};\n\nvoid floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,\n                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path,\n                   const size_t numNodes,\n                   const Blocks& rowBlocks, const Blocks& colBlocks,\n                   const Grid& grid) {\n    if (numNodes == 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t rowStart = static_cast<size_t>(rowBlocks.offsets[grid.myRow]);\n    const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(rowBlocks.counts[grid.myRow]);\n    const size_t colStart = static_cast<size_t>(colBlocks.offsets[grid.myCol]);\n    const size_t colCount = static_cast<size_t>(colBlocks.counts[grid.myCol]);\n\n    // Double buffers for the pivot row/column slices (current and prefetched).\n    std::vector<unsigned int> rowBuf(2 * colCount), colBuf(2 * rowCount);\n    unsigned int* curRow = rowBuf.data();\n    unsigned int* nextRow = rowBuf.data() + colCount;\n    unsigned int* curCol = colBuf.data();\n    unsigned int* nextCol = colBuf.data() + rowCount;\n\n    const int rowLen = static_cast<int>(colCount);\n    const int colLen = static_cast<int>(rowCount);\n\n    MPI_Request reqs[2];\n\n    // Fills the pivot slices for iteration `k` from local data (only the owning\n    // ranks contribute) and starts the two broadcasts.\n    auto postPivot = [&](const size_t k, unsigned int* rowDst, unsigned int* colDst) {\n        const int kr = rowBlocks.owner(k);\n        const int kc = colBlocks.owner(k);\n        if (grid.myRow == kr) {\n            std::memcpy(rowDst, dist.data() + (k - rowStart) * colCount,\n                        colCount * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n        if (grid.myCol == kc) {\n            const size_t lc = k - colStart;\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < rowCount; ++i) {\n                colDst[i] = dist[i * colCount + lc];\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Ibcast(rowDst, rowLen, MPI_UNSIGNED, kr, grid.colComm, &reqs[0]);\n        MPI_Ibcast(colDst, colLen, MPI_UNSIGNED, kc, grid.rowComm, &reqs[1]);\n    };\n\n    postPivot(0, curRow, curCol);\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n        MPI_Waitall(2, reqs, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);\n        const unsigned int ku = static_cast<unsigned int>(k);\n\n        const size_t kNext = k + 1;\n        const bool haveNext = kNext < numNodes;\n        const int nr = haveNext ? rowBlocks.owner(kNext) : 0;\n        const int nc = haveNext ? colBlocks.owner(kNext) : 0;\n        const bool rowNextMine = haveNext && grid.myRow == nr;\n        const bool colNextMine = haveNext && grid.myCol == nc;\n        const size_t pivotLocalRow = rowNextMine ? kNext - rowStart : 0;\n\n        // Produce the pivot data of the next iteration first, so its broadcasts\n        // can be posted before the (much larger) bulk update.  Relaxation with a\n        // fixed pivot is idempotent, so the overlap between the row and the\n        // column update below is harmless.\n        if (rowNextMine) {\n            relaxRow(dist.data() + pivotLocalRow * colCount, path.data() + pivotLocalRow * colCount,\n                     curRow, colCount, curCol[pivotLocalRow], ku);\n            std::memcpy(nextRow, dist.data() + pivotLocalRow * colCount,\n                        colCount * sizeof(unsigned int));\n        }\n        if (colNextMine) {\n            const size_t lc = kNext - colStart;\n            const unsigned int distKJ = curRow[lc];\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < rowCount; ++i) {\n                const size_t off = i * colCount + lc;\n                const unsigned int newDist = curCol[i] + distKJ;\n                if (newDist < dist[off]) {\n                    dist[off] = newDist;\n                    path[off] = ku;\n                }\n                nextCol[i] = dist[off];\n            }\n        }\n        if (haveNext) {\n            MPI_Ibcast(nextRow, rowLen, MPI_UNSIGNED, nr, grid.colComm, &reqs[0]);\n            MPI_Ibcast(nextCol, colLen, MPI_UNSIGNED, nc, grid.rowComm, &reqs[1]);\n        }\n\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < rowCount; ++i) {\n            if (rowNextMine && i == pivotLocalRow) {\n                continue;  // already relaxed above\n            }\n            relaxRow(dist.data() + i * colCount, path.data() + i * colCount, curRow, colCount,\n                     curCol[i], ku);\n        }\n\n        std::swap(curRow, nextRow);\n        std::swap(curCol, nextCol);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, const size_t numNodes) {\n    // Basic sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Diagonal should be zero\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {\n        if (dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] != 0) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: diagonal element [%zu,%zu] is not zero\\n\", i, i);\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // 2. Triangle inequality: dist[i][k] + dist[k][j] >= dist[i][j]\n    // Check a sample of paths to avoid O(n^3) validation time\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < std::min(numNodes, static_cast<size_t>(10)); ++j) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {\n                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];\n                const unsigned int distKJ = dist[idx2(j, k, numNodes)];\n\n                // Check for overflow before addition\n                if (distIK < INF && distKJ < INF) {\n                    if (distIK + distKJ < distIJ) {\n                        printf(\"Validation failed: triangle inequality violated at [%zu,%zu,%zu]\\n\",\n                               i, j, k);\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of nodes in the graph (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t numNodes = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numNodes = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Build the process grid: as square as possible, but never with more parts\n    // than there are nodes in either dimension.\n    Grid grid;\n    {\n        int pr = 1;\n        for (int cand = static_cast<int>(std::sqrt(static_cast<double>(numRanks))); cand >= 1;\n             --cand) {\n            if (numRanks % cand == 0) {\n                pr = cand;\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        grid.pr = pr;\n        grid.pc = numRanks / pr;\n        // Keep both dimensions non-degenerate for very small problems.\n        while (grid.pr > 1 && static_cast<size_t>(grid.pr) > numNodes) {\n            grid.pr /= 2;\n            grid.pc = numRanks / grid.pr;\n        }\n        grid.myRow = rank / grid.pc;\n        grid.myCol = rank % grid.pc;\n        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, grid.myRow, grid.myCol, &grid.rowComm);\n        MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, grid.myCol, grid.myRow, &grid.colComm);\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Floyd-Warshall All-Pairs Shortest Path Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of nodes: %zu\\n\", numNodes);\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d (process grid %d x %d)\\n\", numRanks, grid.pr, grid.pc);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    const Blocks rowBlocks(numNodes, grid.pr);\n    const Blocks colBlocks(numNodes, grid.pc);\n    const size_t rowStart = static_cast<size_t>(rowBlocks.offsets[grid.myRow]);\n    const size_t rowCount = static_cast<size_t>(rowBlocks.counts[grid.myRow]);\n    const size_t colStart = static_cast<size_t>(colBlocks.offsets[grid.myCol]);\n    const size_t colCount = static_cast<size_t>(colBlocks.counts[grid.myCol]);\n\n    // Allocate the local sub-block of both matrices\n    std::vector<unsigned int> dist(rowCount * colCount);\n    std::vector<unsigned int> path(rowCount * colCount);\n\n    // Initialize\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Initializing graph...\\n\");\n    }\n    initializeDistanceMatrix(dist, numNodes, rowStart, rowCount, colStart, colCount, 1,\n                             MAX_DISTANCE);\n    initializePathMatrix(path, rowStart, rowCount, colCount);\n\n    // Run Floyd-Warshall\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computing shortest paths...\\n\");\n    }\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    floydWarshall(dist, path, numNodes, rowBlocks, colBlocks, grid);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    long long elapsed = static_cast<long long>(duration.count());\n    MPI_Allreduce(MPI_IN_PLACE, &elapsed, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computation time: %lld ms\\n\", elapsed);\n\n        // Calculate operations per second\n        // Floyd-Warshall has O(n³) complexity\n        double ops = (double)numNodes * numNodes * numNodes;\n        double gflops = ops / (elapsed / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    }\n\n    // Collect the full distance matrix on rank 0 for output / validation\n    std::vector<unsigned int> fullDist;\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        std::vector<int> counts(numRanks), displs(numRanks);\n        int off = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n            counts[r] = rowBlocks.counts[r / grid.pc] * colBlocks.counts[r % grid.pc];\n            displs[r] = off;\n            off += counts[r];\n        }\n        std::vector<unsigned int> gathered;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            gathered.resize(numNodes * numNodes);\n        }\n        MPI_Gatherv(dist.data(), static_cast<int>(dist.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED,\n                    rank == 0 ? gathered.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), displs.data(),\n                    MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            fullDist.resize(numNodes * numNodes);\n            for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n                const int br = r / grid.pc, bc = r % grid.pc;\n                const size_t rs = static_cast<size_t>(rowBlocks.offsets[br]);\n                const size_t rc = static_cast<size_t>(rowBlocks.counts[br]);\n                const size_t cs = Rank `(r,c)` owns a contiguous row block × column block of both matrices; nothing is replicated. Two sub-communicators are split out: one per process row, one per process column.\n- **Per pivot `k`**: the process row owning `k` broadcasts its slice of row `k` down the process columns (`n/pc` elements over `pr` ranks), and the process column owning `k` broadcasts its slice of column `k` along the process rows (`n/pr` over `pc`). This is what makes it scale — a 1D row decomposition needs a full `n`-element broadcast over all `P` ranks each step.\n- **Communication/computation overlap**: the pivot row and column for iteration `k+1` are relaxed first, then their `MPI_Ibcast`es are posted, and only then is the bulk of the local block updated. Relaxation against a fixed pivot is idempotent, so the row/column pre-updates can overlap safely.\n- **Kernel**: conditional store (`if (newDist < d[j])`, with an unlikely hint) rather than a branch-free vectorized select. Measured: improvements become rare after the first pivots, so keeping cache lines clean beats vectorizing — 245 vs 116 GOPS at 128 ranks.\n- Init generates the identical `rand_r` stream on every rank but stores only the local block, so no scatter is needed.
